# Drive files the Sadhana app depends on

Checked 23 Oct 2026 (prompt-f6), read-only: live D1 links + Drive metadata via the read-only service account. Nothing was changed.

## What this means

- The app's database holds the **catalogue** (book titles, chapters, lecture titles, order) and a **link** to each file. The PDFs and audio themselves live in **Google Drive**, in the accounts below. None is in praveenj811@gmail.com.
- All 1,174 linked files and the 38 lecture folders exist, none is in the trash, and each one could be read without being shared to anyone in particular, so they are shared by link.
- If an owner deletes, moves to trash, or un-shares a file, or the account itself is deleted or locked, that book or lecture stops opening in the app. The app cannot tell; the devotee just gets a Drive error.

## Two warnings first

- The 380 Hindi book and chapter PDFs sit in a Drive folder named **`temp`** (pushpendersinghal@gmail.com, id 1W232SB2ZRMJzYTPt_Id3oSgHrm1v7Xhq). Rename it to something like `IYF Sadhana · Hindi books (used by the app)` so nobody clears it out. Renaming does not break links.
- hearingandreading.4@gmail.com holds 15.6 GB of linked lectures, over the free 15 GB (see item 4).

## Checklist for every account above

1. Know who holds the password and the recovery phone/email; turn on 2-step verification. A lost or locked account takes every file in it out of the app.
2. Never delete, trash or move these files to another account. Moving between accounts changes the file id, and the app's link breaks. Renaming or moving into another folder of the SAME account is safe.
3. Keep each file's sharing at **General access: Anyone with the link · Viewer**. Restricting it makes the app ask devotees to sign in and request access.
4. Watch storage. A free account holds 15 GB. hearingandreading.4 already holds 15.6 GB of linked lectures, so it must have paid storage (Google One): if that subscription lapses, the account goes over quota, and Google may delete the content of an account that stays over quota for 2 years. hearingandreading.2 is at 12 GB. (Sizes count only the files the app links to.)
5. Google may delete a free account after 2 years without any sign-in: sign in to each account at least once a year.
6. Add a new file the same way: upload to one of these accounts, set Anyone with the link · Viewer, then give the link to the app's setup (a migration), never edit links by hand.
